<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-05969:start -->current owner `books/part-07-agent/83-mcp.md` 在 `books/part-07-agent/83-mcp.md#hostclientserver (line 29, H2: Host、Client、Server)` 的实际命题：MCP 使用 client-host-server architecture： **Host** 负责用户体验、模型集成、connection permission、consent 与 security policy。 **Client** 代表 Host 与一个 Server 交互，处理 capability negotiation 和协议消息； 是否存在协议级 session 取决于 revision。 **Server** 暴露 focused capabilities，可为 local process 或 remote service。 一个 client-server interaction 不应自动获得其他 servers 的 Context。Host 是 aggregation 与 isolation 边界。 owner_sha256=e6ec5a4d78decd5fb96ea546ca3855f1cebc2677d38d6961f23c87f6e8d7c539。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-05969:end -->
<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-05969:start -->作者公开的机制是：However, this explosive adoption has exposed a critical gap: the absence of a unified, formal security framework capable of systematically characterizing, analyzing, and mitigating the diverse threats facing MCP-based agent ecosystems. Existing security research remains fragmented across individual attack papers, isolated benchmarks, and point defense mechanisms. 对系统而言，MCP host/runtime 拥有 lifecycle、server admission、capability 与 receipt；身份或 policy 不满足时拒绝连接并回退受限工具集。 exact-v1 Method evidence: VI MCPShield: Defense-in-Depth Architecture Based on the coverage gap analysis, we propose MCPShield, a layered security architecture that integrates four complementary defense layers to achieve comprehensive coverage across all seven threat categories. VI-A Architectural Overview MCPShield follows a defense-in-depth strategy inspired by zero trust principles [26] and capability-based security [10]. The architecture consists of four layers: 1. Layer 1: Capability-Based Access Control (L-CAC). Governs what each agent can do by restricting tool invocations to explicitly granted capabilities. 2. Layer 2: Cryptographic Tool Attestation (L-CTA). Ensures what each tool is by verifying tool identity, integrity, and pr<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-05969:end --> Decision=`No Change — Existing Coverage`；evidence boundary：`A Formal Security Framework for MCP-Based AI Agents: Threat Taxonomy, Verification Models, and Defense Mechanisms` 只证明 exact-v1 的公开模型、数据、硬件和 workload；evaluation evidence 为：Existing security research remains fragmented across individual attack papers, isolated benchmarks, and point defense mechanisms. We make four principal contributions: (1) a hierarchical threat taxonomy comprising 7 threat categories and 23 distinct attack vectors organized across four attack surfaces, grounded in the analysis of over 177000 MCP tools; (2) a formal verification model based on labeled transition systems with trust boundary annotations that enables static and runtime analysis of MCP tool interaction chains; (3) a systematic comparative evaluation of 12 existing defense mechanisms, identifying coverage gaps across our threat taxonomy; and (4) a defense in depth reference architecture integrating capability based access control, cryptographic tool attestation, information flow tracking, and runtime policy 未披露生产 SLO、多租户、跨硬件或长期运行结果时不得外推。

<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-06111:start -->current owner `books/part-06-ai-infrastructure/66-evaluation-system.md` 在 `books/part-06-ai-infrastructure/66-evaluation-system.md#本章要回答的问题 (line 10, H2: 本章要回答的问题)` 的实际命题：为什么训练 loss、benchmark 分数、用户点赞和系统 SLO 都不能单独证明一个 AI System “更好”？Evaluation 应评估模型、完整请求路径，还是 Agent trajectory？离线评估、线上实验与生产反馈怎样形成一个可审计的发布控制回路？ 本章的核心判断是：**Evaluation System 是把目标转化为可重复证据和受控决策的系统。它必须同时版本化被评估对象、输入分布、执行环境与 scorer，并显式表达不确定性、切片和风险；工具可以保存证据，但不能替组织定义什么算成功。** > 时效边界：本章描述长期稳定的评估对象与控制回路，不把某个 benchmark、judge model 或平台 API 当作 Evaluation 的定义。MLflow 的实现映射按 2026 年 7 月官方文档核验；其 classic ML 与 GenAI evaluation 接口仍在演进，生产使用必须锁定实际版本。 owner_sha256=ba07952c1fe646f17bfd554f905fb4a594aaa8676cc3e8aea0edfa2526800e09。<!-- existing:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-06111:end -->
<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-06111:start -->作者公开的机制是：To address these issues, we propose AgentCE-Bench built around a unified grid-based planning task, where agents must fill hidden slots in a partially completed schedule subject to both local slot constraints and global constraints. 对系统而言，evaluation system 拥有 dataset/environment/scorer/run/decision identity；不可复算、校准失败或 slice 退化时保留旧 release 并升级人工复核。 exact-v1 Method evidence: 3 Dataset Construction 3.1 Overview and Task Formulation. Each task instance consists of an grid where each cell must be filled with one item from a domain-specific pool . A subset of cells are hidden slots that the agent must fill; the rest are pre-filled. Each hidden slot is subject to slot constraints (local attribute restrictions) and all slots must jointly satisfy global constraints over the entire grid. Each hidden slot provides candidates, which fall into three categories: the truth (unique correct answer), filter candidates (violate , eliminable by local reasoning), and decoy candidates (satisfy but violate under any valid completion), ensuring a unique solution per instance. Example shown in Figure 6.<!-- delta:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-06111:end --> Decision=`No Change — Existing Coverage`；evidence boundary：`AgentCE-Bench: Agent Configurable Evaluation with Scalable Horizons and Controllable Difficulty under Lightweight Environments` 只证明 exact-v1 的公开模型、数据、硬件和 workload；evaluation evidence 为：Crucially, all tool calls are resolved via static JSON files under a \textbf{Lightweight Environment} design, eliminating setup overhead and enabling fast, reproducible evaluation suitable for training-time validation. We then conduct comprehensive experiments across 13 models of diverse sizes and families over 6 domains, revealing significant cross-model performance variation and confirming that AgentCE-Bench provides interpretable and controllable evaluation of agent reasoning. 未披露生产 SLO、多租户、跨硬件或长期运行结果时不得外推。
<!-- books-review:SF-2026-ARXIV-2604-06111:end -->
